1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the stability structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 192. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 163 and converges at rate O(1/n^1).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on stability structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 192. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 163. QED.
